				local t = O:FindFirstChildOfClass(Eg(-894594 + 833678));

					end;

		end;

		RegisterHit:FireServer(O[104850 + -104849][3181353980 % 12776522], O);
	end);
end;

function updateESP(...)

		return;
	end;
	k = Y;

		return;

			local Y = O:GetAttribute(Eg(167674 - 228507)) or O[Eg(-702382 + 640812)] == Eg(-891154 + 830314) or O[Eg(-301601 + 240031)] == Eg(859220 + -920894) or O[Eg(46664 - 108234)] == Eg(-208501 + 147367) or O[Eg(292743 + -354313)] == Eg(988354 + -1049495) or O[Eg(814072 - 875642)] == Eg(206827 + -268142);

				end;

function EnableESP(...)
	if s then
		return;
	end;
	s = true;
	for Y, O in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		createESP(O);
	end;
	task[Eg(-225594 - (-164613))](function(...)
		while s do
			pcall(updateESP);
			task[Eg(819019 - 880578)](D);
		end;
	end);
end;
function DisableESP(...)
	s = false;
	for Y, O in pairs(K) do
		removeESP(Y);
	end;
	K = {};
end;

function getClosestPlayer(Y, ...)

		return nil;
	end;

		local Y = Players:FindFirstChild(AimlockTargetPlayer);
		if Y and (Y[Eg(722202 + -783083)] and Y[Eg(-134752 - (-73871))]:FindFirstChild(Eg(-550595 + 489282))) then
			local O = Y[Eg(-850916 - (-790035))]:FindFirstChildOfClass(Eg(182742 + -243658));

				return Y[Eg(-994442 + 933561)];
			end;
		end;
	end;
	local c, x = nil, math[Eg(360415 - 422056)];
	for Y, t in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if t ~= player and (not BlacklistedPlayers[t[Eg(1027124 + -1088694)]] and (t[Eg(-708911 - (-648030))] and t[Eg(-343608 - (-282727))]:FindFirstChild(Eg(-211441 + 150128)))) then

			if Y and Y[Eg(-837927 - (-776618))] > 865262 + -865262 then
				local Y = ((t[Eg(513131 - 574012)][Eg(273885 - 335198)][Eg(-70255 + 8934)] - O[Eg(646999 - 708320)]))[Eg(104673 + -165923)];
				if Y < x and Y <= z then
					x = Y;
					c = t[Eg(575874 + -636755)];

		end;
	end;
	return c;
end;

function ClearAura(...)
	for Y, O in pairs(AuraObjects) do
		if O and O[Eg(464693 + -525791)] then
			O:Destroy();
		end;
	end;
	AuraObjects = {};
end;

function performExtendedSoru(Y, ...)

		return;

		return;
	end;

		pcall(function(...)
			local O = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(Eg(-1034348 + 972673)) and ReplicatedStorage[Eg(-782352 - (-720677))]:FindFirstChild(Eg(-455527 + 393865));

				O:InvokeServer(Eg(117006 - 178355), Y);
			else
				z[Eg(711524 + -772811)] = CFrame[Eg(-812333 - (-751304))](Y);
			end;
		end);
	else
		local O = math[Eg(-285594 - (-224487))](x / (-4162 - (-5062)));
		local t = ((Y - c))[Eg(-170988 + 109696)];
		for Y = -122509 - (-122510), O, 1027011 + -1027010 do
			local O = math[Eg(127449 + -188773)](Y * (-578306 + 579206), x);

				local Y = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(Eg(860715 - 922390)) and ReplicatedStorage[Eg(-860914 - (-799239))]:FindFirstChild(Eg(431266 - 492928));

				end;
			end);
			task[Eg(-278981 + 217422)](.015);
		end;
	end;
end;

function IsSilentAimAlly(Y, ...)

	local z = O and (O:FindFirstChild(Eg(-217454 + 156344)) and (O[Eg(-677211 + 616101)]:FindFirstChild(Eg(-693379 + 632074)) and (O[Eg(-572325 + 511215)][Eg(143542 + -204847)]:FindFirstChild(Eg(-1004673 - (-943563))) and (O[Eg(369742 + -430852)][Eg(268035 + -329340)][Eg(959544 - 1020654)]:FindFirstChild(Eg(-206166 + 144804)) and O[Eg(543942 - 605052)][Eg(-381167 + 319862)][Eg(-462318 - (-401208))][Eg(-1108811 - (-1047449))]:FindFirstChild(Eg(-139583 + 78158))))));
	if not z then
		return false;
	end;
	return z:FindFirstChild(Y[Eg(-75967 - (-14397))]) ~= nil;
end;
function IsSilentAimEnemy(Y, ...)
	if not Y or Y == player then
		return false;
	end;
	if IsSilentAimAlly(Y) then
		return false;
	end;
	local O, z = player[Eg(-553680 - (-492243))], Y[Eg(-634174 - (-572737))];
	if O and (z and (O[Eg(-1095468 - (-1033898))] == Eg(429281 + -490196) and z[Eg(-960852 + 899282)] == Eg(369016 - 429931))) then
		return false;
	end;
	return true;
end;

function GetClosestTargetToCenter(...)
	local Y = player[Eg(-182251 + 121370)];
	local O = Y and Y:FindFirstChild(Eg(289057 - 350370));
	if not O then
		return nil;
	end;
	local z = workspace[Eg(-56691 + -4955)];
	if not z then
		return nil;
	end;
	local c = Vector2[Eg(711097 - 772126)](z[Eg(-746604 - (-684953))][Eg(47941 - 109178)] / (-1019880 - (-1019882)), z[Eg(-1034608 - (-972957))][Eg(-826728 - (-765643))] / (-385509 - (-385511)));
	local x = _G[Eg(461658 + -523124)] or 1955111912 % 14375822;
	local t = maxRange or 793830 - 791330;
	if _G[Eg(-900132 + 838455)] and (_G[Eg(818225 + -879902)] ~= Eg(-502160 - (-441003)) and _G[Eg(-310516 - (-248839))] ~= Eg(-691306 + 630364)) then
		local Y = Players:FindFirstChild(_G[Eg(-521867 - (-460190))]);
		if Y and (Y ~= player and (Y[Eg(-345723 + 284842)] and not BlacklistedPlayers[Y[Eg(-30046 + -31524)]])) then
			if not _G[Eg(-115066 - (-53856))] or IsSilentAimEnemy(Y) then
				local d = Y[Eg(-742046 - (-681165))]:FindFirstChildOfClass(Eg(342948 - 403864));
				local y = Y[Eg(-97914 - (-37033))]:FindFirstChild(Eg(40346 - 101659));
				if d and (d[Eg(561899 - 623208)] > -895296 - (-895296) and y) then
					local d = ((y[Eg(-750303 + 688982)] - O[Eg(468257 - 529578)]))[Eg(13186 + -74436)];
					if d <= t then
						local O, t = z:WorldToViewportPoint(y[Eg(149062 + -210383)]);
						local d = ((Vector2[Eg(-505498 + 444469)](O[Eg(-609717 + 548480)], O[Eg(295499 + -356584)]) - c))[Eg(-162116 + 100866)];
						if t and d <= x then
							return Y[Eg(-830470 + 769589)]:FindFirstChild(_G[Eg(376810 - 438443)]) or y;
						end;
					end;
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	local d, y = nil, t;
	local function M(Y, ...)
		if not Y then
			return;
		end;
		local z = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Y);
		if z and BlacklistedPlayers[z[Eg(-216991 + 155421)]] then
			return;
		end;
		local c = Y:FindFirstChildOfClass(Eg(-258593 + 197677));
		if not c or c[Eg(1011727 + -1073036)] <= 1049252400 % 13990032 then
			return;
		end;
		local x = Y:FindFirstChild(_G[Eg(-754868 - (-693235))]) or Y:FindFirstChild(Eg(555702 + -617015));
		if not x then
			return;
		end;
		local t = ((x[Eg(-865133 + 803812)] - O[Eg(-246108 - (-184787))]))[Eg(-113365 + 52115)];
		if t < y then
			y = t;
			d = x;
		end;
	end;
	if _G[Eg(445258 + -506587)] or SilentAimPlayersEnabled then
		for Y, O in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if O ~= player and not BlacklistedPlayers[O[Eg(-1055886 - (-994316))]] then
				if not _G[Eg(387768 - 448978)] or IsSilentAimEnemy(O) then
					M(O[Eg(-87070 + 26189)]);
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	if ((_G[Eg(-217400 - (-156058))] or SilentAimNPCsEnabled)) and workspace:FindFirstChild(Eg(-991943 - (-930424))) then
		for Y, O in ipairs(workspace[Eg(-132251 + 70732)]:GetChildren()) do
			M(O);
		end;
	end;
	return d;
end;

function formatNumber(Y, ...)
	if type(Y) ~= Eg(-339376 - (-277842)) then
		return tostring(Y);
	end;
	local O = tostring(Y);

		local Y;
		O, Y = string[Eg(-278417 + 217028)](O, Eg(938172 + -999027), Eg(-892221 - (-831131)));
		if Y == 1003082 + -1003082 then
			break;
		end;
	end;
	return O;
end;
function getGameStat(Y, ...)
	local O = nil;
	local z = player:FindFirstChild(Eg(977038 + -1038256));
	if z then
		for z, c in ipairs(z:GetChildren()) do
			if string[Eg(841999 + -902889)](c[Eg(262059 - 323629)]) == string[Eg(759144 - 820034)](Y) or string[Eg(-870607 - (-808986))](string[Eg(711297 - 772187)](c[Eg(136896 - 198466)]), string[Eg(-94285 - (-33395))](Y)) then
				O = c[Eg(-868757 - (-807189))];
				break;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	if O == nil then
		local z = player:FindFirstChild(Eg(628320 + -689416));
		if z then
			for z, c in ipairs(z:GetChildren()) do
				if string[Eg(-839730 - (-778840))](c[Eg(782754 - 844324)]) == string[Eg(-131157 + 70267)](Y) or string[Eg(-1022798 - (-961177))](string[Eg(-354202 + 293312)](c[Eg(-468461 + 406891)]), string[Eg(-1031868 + 970978)](Y)) then
					O = c[Eg(27526 - 89094)];
					break;
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	if O == nil then
		local z = player:GetAttribute(Y);
		if z ~= nil then
			O = z;
		end;
	end;
	return O;
end;

function executeSoruCombo(...)
	if not MacroEnabled or MacroMode ~= Eg(-574393 + 513271) or MacroExecuting then
		return;
	end;
	MacroExecuting = true;
	local Y = {
			[553558 - 553557] = Enum[Eg(-272821 + 211141)][Eg(-595466 + 534491)],
			[-627875 + 627877] = Enum[Eg(-964333 + 902653)][Eg(-161451 - (-99906))],
			[912419 - 912416] = Enum[Eg(194914 + -256594)][Eg(285235 + -346482)],
			[419838 - 419834] = Enum[Eg(267787 + -329467)][Eg(-391392 + 329758)],
		};
	local function O(Y, ...)
		VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Y, false, game);
		task[Eg(418247 + -479806)](.08);
		VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Y, false, game);
	end;
	local function z(z, ...)
		O(Y[z]);
		task[Eg(-71924 + 10365)](.12);
		local c = player[Eg(-202725 + 141844)] and player[Eg(-294019 - (-233138))]:FindFirstChildOfClass(Eg(-369561 - (-308724)));
		if not c then
			O(Y[z]);
			task[Eg(-602921 - (-541362))](.1);
		end;
	end;
	pcall(function(...)
		z(MacroSlot1);
		task[Eg(-789303 + 727744)](.06);
		O(Enum[Eg(-958856 + 897176)][MacroKey1]);
		task[Eg(438519 - 500078)](MacroDelay);
		if MacroSlot1 ~= MacroSlot2 then
			z(MacroSlot2);
			task[Eg(655516 + -717075)](.06);
		end;
		O(Enum[Eg(525017 - 586697)][MacroKey2]);
	end);
	MacroExecuting = false;
end;
